    db26:2-en 
    Which actor appeared in the most
    films in a single decade?
    		 
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

    SELECT ?actor
    WHERE {
      {
        SELECT ?actor ((FLOOR(?year/10)*10) AS ?decade) ?film
        WHERE {
          ?film a dbo:Film ;
                dbo:starring ?actor ;
                dbo:releaseDate ?date .
          BIND (xsd:integer(SUBSTR(STR(?date),1,4)) AS ?year)
        }
      }
    }
    GROUP BY ?actor ?decade
    ORDER BY DESC(COUNT(DISTINCT ?film))
    LIMIT 1
    		 
    {
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Mithun_Chakraborty": 1
    }
    		 
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT ?actor ?actorLabel ?decade (COUNT(DISTINCT ?film) AS ?count) WHERE {
      ?film rdf:type ?ft .
      ?film rdfs:label ?filmLabel . FILTER(lang(?filmLabel)='en')
      FILTER(regex(str(?ft),"Film|Work","i"))

      { ?film dbo:starring ?actor } UNION { ?film dbp:starring ?actor }
      ?actor rdfs:label ?actorLabel . FILTER(lang(?actorLabel)='en')

      OPTIONAL { ?film dbo:releaseDate ?rdate }
      OPTIONAL { ?film dbp:released ?rdate }
      OPTIONAL { ?film dbp:releaseDate ?rdate }
      OPTIONAL { ?film dbp:releasedYear ?ryear }

      BIND(COALESCE(STR(?rdate), STR(?ryear), "") AS ?rstr)
      FILTER(regex(?rstr, "\\d{4}"))
      BIND(xsd:integer(REPLACE(?rstr, "^.*?(\\d{4}).*$", "$1")) AS ?yr)
      BIND(xsd:integer(FLOOR(?yr / 10) * 10) AS ?decade)
    }
    GROUP BY ?actor ?actorLabel ?decade
    ORDER BY DESC(?count)
    LIMIT 1
    		 
    {}
    		 
    {
        "set_P": 0.0,
        "set_recall": 0.0,
        "set_F": 0.0
    }
    db26:2-es 
    ¿Qué actor apareció en la mayor
    cantidad de películas en una sola
    década?
    		 
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

    SELECT ?actor
    WHERE {
      {
        SELECT ?actor ((FLOOR(?year/10)*10) AS ?decade) ?film
        WHERE {
          ?film a dbo:Film ;
                dbo:starring ?actor ;
                dbo:releaseDate ?date .
          BIND (xsd:integer(SUBSTR(STR(?date),1,4)) AS ?year)
        }
      }
    }
    GROUP BY ?actor ?decade
    ORDER BY DESC(COUNT(DISTINCT ?film))
    LIMIT 1
    		 
    {
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Mithun_Chakraborty": 1
    }
    		 
    SELECT ?actor (SAMPLE(?actorLabel) AS ?actorLabelSample) ?decade (COUNT(DISTINCT ?film) AS ?filmsCount) WHERE {
      VALUES ?starringProp { dbo:starring dbp:starring dbo:starring } 
      ?film a dbo:Film .
      ?film ?starringProp ?actor .
      ?actor a dbo:Actor .
      OPTIONAL { ?actor rdfs:label ?actorLabel FILTER(lang(?actorLabel) IN ("es","en")) }

      # Try several date properties and year literals
      OPTIONAL { ?film dbo:releaseDate ?date1 }
      OPTIONAL { ?film dbp:releaseDate ?date2 }
      OPTIONAL { ?film dbp:year ?yearLit }
      OPTIONAL { ?film dbo:year ?yearLit2 }

      BIND(COALESCE(?date1, ?date2, ?yearLit, ?yearLit2) AS ?d)
      # extract year robustly: use YEAR() when possible, otherwise take first 4 chars
      BIND(
        IF(
          (datatype(?d) = xsd:date || datatype(?d) = xsd:dateTime),
          YEAR(?d),
          IF(regex(str(?d), "^\\d{4}"), xsd:integer(substr(str(?d),1,4)), null)
        ) AS ?yr
      )
      FILTER(bound(?yr))
      BIND( (FLOOR(?yr/10) * 10) AS ?decade )
    }
    GROUP BY ?actor ?decade
    ORDER BY DESC(?filmsCount)
    LIMIT 1
    		 
    {}

    db26:3-en 
    Who (either an actor or a musician)
    has received the highest number of
    awards?
    		 
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>

    SELECT ?person
    WHERE {
      {
        ?person a dbo:Actor .
      }
      UNION
      {
        ?person a dbo:MusicalArtist .
      }

      ?person dbo:award ?award .
    }
    GROUP BY ?person
    ORDER BY DESC(COUNT(DISTINCT ?award))
    LIMIT 1
    		 
    {
        "http://es.dbpedia.org/resource/Federico_Luppi": 1
    }
    		 
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT ?person (COUNT(DISTINCT ?award) AS ?awardCount) (SAMPLE(?lab) AS ?label) WHERE {
      ?person rdf:type dbo:Person .
      FILTER ( EXISTS { ?person rdf:type dbo:Actor } || EXISTS { ?person rdf:type dbr:Musician } || EXISTS { ?person rdf:type dbo:MusicalArtist } )
      ?person (dbo:award|dbp:award) ?award .
      OPTIONAL { ?person rdfs:label ?lab . FILTER(lang(?lab) = 'en') }
    } GROUP BY ?person ORDER BY DESC(?awardCount) LIMIT 1

    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT ?person (COUNT(DISTINCT ?award) AS ?awardsCount) WHERE {
      ?person a dbo:Person .
      VALUES ?type { dbo:Actor dbo:MusicalArtist dbo:Musician }
      ?person a ?type .
      { ?person dbo:award ?award } UNION { ?person dbp:award ?award }
    } GROUP BY ?person
    ORDER BY DESC(?awardsCount)
    LIMIT 1

    db26:4-en 
    Which are the 5 bands or musicians
    who have produced the highest
    number of albums?
    		 
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>

    SELECT ?artist
    WHERE {
      {
        ?artist a dbo:MusicalArtist .
      }
      UNION
      {
        ?artist a dbo:Band .
      }

      ?album dbo:artist ?artist .
    }
    GROUP BY ?artist
    ORDER BY DESC(COUNT(DISTINCT ?album))
    LIMIT 5
    		 
    {
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/The_Beatles": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Merzbow": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Ilaiyaraaja": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Grateful_Dead": 1,

    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>

    PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT ?artist (SAMPLE(?name) AS ?label) (COUNT(DISTINCT ?album) AS ?albums) WHERE {
      ?album a dbo:Album ;
             (dbo:artist|dbp:artist) ?artist .
      ?artist rdfs:label ?name FILTER(lang(?name)='en').
      FILTER(
        EXISTS { ?artist rdf:type dbo:Band } ||
        EXISTS { ?artist rdf:type dbo:MusicalArtist } ||
        EXISTS { ?artist dbo:occupation dbr:Musician }
      )
    }
    GROUP BY ?artist
    ORDER BY DESC(?albums)
    LIMIT 5

    db26:4-es 
    ¿Cuáles son las 5 bandas o músicos
    que han producido el mayor número
    de álbumes?
    		 
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>

    SELECT ?artist
    WHERE {
      {
        ?artist a dbo:MusicalArtist .
      }
      UNION
      {
        ?artist a dbo:Band .
      }

      ?album dbo:artist ?artist .
    }
    GROUP BY ?artist
    ORDER BY DESC(COUNT(DISTINCT ?album))
    LIMIT 5
    		 
    {
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/The_Beatles": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Merzbow": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Ilaiyaraaja": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Grateful_Dead": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/The_Rolling_Stones": 1
    }
    		 
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT ?artist (SAMPLE(?artistLabel) AS ?label) (COUNT(DISTINCT ?album) AS ?albumCount) WHERE {
      ?album a dbo:Album .
      OPTIONAL { ?album dbo:artist ?artist . }
      OPTIONAL { ?album dbp:artist ?artist . }
      FILTER(BOUND(?artist))
      OPTIONAL { ?artist rdfs:label ?artistLabel . FILTER(lang(?artistLabel) = 'en') }
    } GROUP BY ?artist
    ORDER BY DESC(?albumCount)
    LIMIT 5

    db26:5-en 
    Which are the top 3 taxonomic
    kingdoms that have the largest
    number of endangered species?
    		 
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>

    SELECT ?kingdom
    WHERE {
      {
        SELECT ?species ?kingdom
        WHERE {
          ?species a dbo:Species ;
                  dbo:conservationStatus ?status ;
                  dbo:kingdom ?kingdom .
          FILTER (regex(str(?status), "Endangered|Critically endangered", "i"))
        }
      }
    }
    GROUP BY ?kingdom
    ORDER BY DESC(COUNT(DISTINCT ?species))
    LIMIT 3
    		 
    {
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Animal": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Plant": 1,
        "http://es.dbpedia.org/resource/Plantae": 1
    }
    		 
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT ?kingdomIRI (SAMPLE(?kingdomLabel) AS ?kingdomLabel) (COUNT(DISTINCT ?species) AS ?count) WHERE {
      ?species (dbo:conservationStatus|dbp:conservationStatus) ?status .
      ?species (dbo:kingdom|dbp:kingdom) ?kingdomIRI .
      OPTIONAL { ?kingdomIRI rdfs:label ?kingdomLabel . FILTER(lang(?kingdomLabel) = 'en') }
      FILTER( regex(lcase(str(?status)), "endanger") || lcase(str(?status)) = "en" )
    }
    GROUP BY ?kingdomIRI
    ORDER BY DESC(?count)
    LIMIT 3

    ¿Cuáles son los 3 reinos
    taxonómicos con el mayor número de
    especies en peligro de extinción?
    		 
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>

    SELECT ?kingdom
    WHERE {
      {
        SELECT ?species ?kingdom
        WHERE {
          ?species a dbo:Species ;
                  dbo:conservationStatus ?status ;
                  dbo:kingdom ?kingdom .
          FILTER (regex(str(?status), "Endangered|Critically endangered", "i"))
        }
      }
    }
    GROUP BY ?kingdom
    ORDER BY DESC(COUNT(DISTINCT ?species))
    LIMIT 3
    		 
    {
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Animal": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Plant": 1,
        "http://es.dbpedia.org/resource/Plantae": 1
    }

    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
    SELECT ?kingdom (COUNT(DISTINCT ?s) AS ?count) WHERE {
      ?s rdf:type dbo:Species .
      ?s (dbo:conservationStatus|dbp:conservationStatus|dbo:iucnCategory|dbp:iucnCategory) ?status .
      ?s dbo:kingdom ?kingdom .
      FILTER(regex(lcase(str(?status)), "endang"))
    }
    GROUP BY ?kingdom
    ORDER BY DESC(?count)
    LIMIT 3

        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Animal": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Plant": 1,

    db26:10-en 
    Is Blender released under the GNU
    license?
    		 
    ASK WHERE {
      <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Blender_(software)> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/license> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/GNU_General_Public_License>
    }
    		 
    {
        "true": 1
    }
    		 
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    ASK WHERE {
      VALUES ?s { <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Blender_(software)> }
      {
        ?s dbo:license ?lic .
        FILTER(
          (isIRI(?lic) && EXISTS { ?lic rdfs:label ?l . FILTER(lang(?l)='en' && (regex(?l,'GNU','i') || regex(?l,'GPL','i') || regex(?l,'General Public License','i'))) })
        || (!isIRI(?lic) && (regex(str(?lic),'GNU','i') || regex(str(?lic),'GPL','i') || regex(str(?lic),'General Public License','i')))
        )
      }
      UNION
      {
        ?s dbp:license ?lic2 .
        FILTER(
          (isIRI(?lic2) && EXISTS { ?lic2 rdfs:label ?l2 . FILTER(lang(?l2)='en' && (regex(?l2,'GNU','i') || regex(?l2,'GPL','i') || regex(?l2,'General Public License','i'))) })
        || (!isIRI(?lic2) && (regex(str(?lic2),'GNU','i') || regex(str(?lic2),'GPL','i') || regex(str(?lic2),'General Public License','i')))
        )
      }
    }
    		 
    {
        "true": 1
    }
    		 
    {
        "set_P": 1.0,
        "set_recall": 1.0,
        "set_F": 1.0
    }
    db26:10-es 
    ¿Blender se publica bajo la
    licencia GNU?
    		 
    ASK WHERE {
      <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Blender_(software)> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/license> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/GNU_General_Public_License>
    }
    		 
    {
        "true": 1
    }
    		 
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    ASK WHERE {
      VALUES ?sw { <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Blender_(software)> }
      {
        ?sw dbo:license dbr:GNU_General_Public_License .
      } UNION {
        ?sw dbp:license dbr:GNU_General_Public_License .
      } UNION {
        ?sw dbo:license ?lic . ?lic rdfs:label ?l . FILTER(langMatches(lang(?l), "EN") && regex(?l, "GNU", "i"))
      } UNION {
        ?sw dbp:license ?licLit . FILTER(isLiteral(?licLit) && regex(str(?licLit), "GNU", "i"))
      } UNION {
        ?sw dbo:license ?licIRI . FILTER(isIRI(?licIRI)) . ?licIRI rdfs:label ?ll . FILTER(regex(?ll, "GNU", "i"))
      }
    }
    		 
    {
        "true": 1

    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?work ?label ?pubDate WHERE {
      {
        ?work dbo:author dbr:Mark_Twain .
      } UNION {
        ?work dbp:author dbr:Mark_Twain .
      } UNION {
        ?work dbo:writer dbr:Mark_Twain .
      } UNION {
        ?work dbp:writer dbr:Mark_Twain .
      }
      OPTIONAL { ?work dbo:publicationDate ?pd1 }
      OPTIONAL { ?work dbp:published ?pd2 }
      OPTIONAL { ?work dbp:publicationDate ?pd3 }
      OPTIONAL { ?work dbo:releaseDate ?pd4 }
      OPTIONAL { ?work dbp:year ?pd5 }
      BIND(COALESCE(?pd1, ?pd2, ?pd3, ?pd4, ?pd5) AS ?pubDate)
      ?work rdfs:label ?label FILTER(lang(?label)='en')
      FILTER(BOUND(?pubDate))
    }
    ORDER BY STR(?pubDate) ?label
    LIMIT 10

    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?work ?labelFinal ?year WHERE {
      VALUES ?mark { dbr:Mark_Twain }
      ?work (dbo:author|dbp:author|dbo:writer|dbp:writer) ?mark .
      OPTIONAL { ?work rdfs:label ?labelEs FILTER(lang(?labelEs) = 'es') }
      OPTIONAL { ?work rdfs:label ?labelEn FILTER(lang(?labelEn) = 'en') }
      BIND(COALESCE(?labelEs, ?labelEn, STRAFTER(STR(?work), '/resource/')) AS ?labelFinal)
      OPTIONAL { ?work dbo:publicationDate ?date }
      OPTIONAL { ?work dbp:published ?date2 }
      OPTIONAL { ?work dbp:pubDate ?date3 }
      OPTIONAL { ?work dbo:releaseDate ?date4 }
      OPTIONAL { ?work dbp:releaseDate ?date5 }
      OPTIONAL { ?work dbo:date ?date6 }
      BIND(COALESCE(?date, ?date2, ?date3, ?date4, ?date5, ?date6) AS ?anyDate)
      BIND(IF(BOUND(?anyDate), SUBSTR(STR(?anyDate),1,4), "") AS ?yearStr)
      FILTER(STRLEN(?yearStr) > 0)
      BIND(xsd:integer(?yearStr) AS ?year)
    }
    ORDER BY ASC(?year) ?labelFinal
    LIMIT 10

    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?char ?charLabel WHERE {
      VALUES ?p { dbo:creator dbp:creator dbo:writer dbp:writer dbp:coCreator dbp:creators }
      ?char ?p dbr:Stan_Lee .
      ?char rdf:type dbo:ComicsCharacter .
      OPTIONAL { ?char rdfs:label ?charLabel FILTER(lang(?charLabel) = 'en') }
    } LIMIT 50

    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?character ?label WHERE { { ?character dbo:creator dbr:Stan_Lee } UNION { ?character dbp:creator dbr:Stan_Lee } OPTIONAL { ?character rdfs:label ?label FILTER(lang(?label) = 'en') } } LIMIT 20

    db26:17-en 
    Stephen Merchant is the executive
    producer of the series having how
    many fictional characters?
    		 
    SELECT (COUNT(DISTINCT ?uri) AS ?count) WHERE {
      ?x <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/executiveProducer> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Stephen_Merchant> .
      ?uri <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/series> ?x .
      ?uri <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#type> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/FictionalCharacter> .
    }
    		 
    {
        "19": 1
    }
    		 
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT ?series (SAMPLE(?seriesLabel) AS ?label) (COUNT(DISTINCT ?char) AS ?numChars) WHERE {
      VALUES ?person { dbr:Stephen_Merchant }
      {
        ?series dbo:executiveProducer ?person .
      } UNION {
        ?series dbp:executiveProducer ?person .
      }
      # Prefer English label
      OPTIONAL { ?series rdfs:label ?seriesLabel FILTER(lang(?seriesLabel) = 'en') }
      # candidate character links/literals
      {
        ?series dbp:characters ?char .
      } UNION {
        ?series dbp:character ?char .
      } UNION {
        ?series dbo:fictionalCharacter ?char .
      } UNION {
        ?series dbo:character ?char .
      } UNION {
        # if starring points to a fictional character resource (typed as dbo:FictionalCharacter)
        ?series dbo:starring ?starring .
        ?starring rdf:type ?starringType .
        FILTER(regex(str(?starringType), "FictionalCharacter","i") || ?starringType = dbo:FictionalCharacter)
        BIND(?starring AS ?char)
      }
    }
    GROUP BY ?series
    ORDER BY DESC(?numChars)
    LIMIT 50
    		 
    {}
    		 
    {
        "set_P": 0.0,
        "set_recall": 0.0,
        "set_F": 0.0
    }

      VALUES ?series { dbr:An_Idiot_Abroad <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Life's_Too_Short> dbr:Lip_Sync_Battle <http://dbpedia.org/resource/The_Office_(U.S._TV_series)> }

      OPTIONAL { ?peak dbp:location ?loc3 . FILTER(regex(str(?loc3), 'Canada', 'i')) BIND('Canada' AS ?countryLabel3) }

    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?player ?playerLabel ?team ?teamLabel WHERE {
      VALUES ?mgrProp { dbo:manager dbp:manager dbo:coach dbp:coach dbo:managerClub dbp:managerClub }
      ?team ?mgrProp dbr:Pep_Guardiola .
      ?player dbo:team ?team .
      ?player rdf:type dbo:SoccerPlayer .

    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?player (COALESCE(?labelEs, ?labelEn, STR(?player)) AS ?label) WHERE {
      # equipos gestionados por Pep Guardiola (varios predicados posibles)
      ?team (dbo:manager|dbp:manager) dbr:Pep_Guardiola .
      # jugadores que han pertenecido a esos equipos (varios predicados posibles)
      ?player (dbo:team|dbp:team) ?team .
      # etiquetas en español o inglés
      OPTIONAL { ?player rdfs:label ?labelEs FILTER(lang(?labelEs) = 'es') }
      OPTIONAL { ?player rdfs:label ?labelEn FILTER(lang(?labelEn) = 'en') }
    }
    ORDER BY ?label

    db26:20-en 
    Through which cities do the rivers
    originating in Yellowstone National
    Park flow?
    		 
    SELECT DISTINCT ?uri WHERE {
      ?x <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/sourcePlace> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Yellowstone_National_Park> .
      ?x <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/city> ?uri .
      ?x <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#type> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/River> .
    }
    		 
    {
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Lewiston,_Idaho": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Tri-Cities,_Washington": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Homedale,_Idaho": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Blackfoot,_Idaho": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Twin_Falls,_Idaho": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Burley,_Idaho": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/American_Falls,_Idaho": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Idaho_Falls,_Idaho": 1
    }
    		 
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?river ?riverLabel ?city ?cityLabel WHERE {
      VALUES ?park { dbr:Yellowstone_National_Park }
      ?river ?srcProp ?park .
      VALUES ?srcProp { dbo:source dbp:source }
      FILTER ( EXISTS { ?river rdf:type dbo:River } || EXISTS { ?river rdfs:label ?rlabel . FILTER(lang(?rlabel)='en' && regex(?rlabel, '\\bRiver\\b','i')) } )
      OPTIONAL {
        ?river ?flowProp ?city .
        VALUES ?flowProp { dbp:flowsThrough dbp:through dbo:location dbp:location }
        ?city rdf:type dbo:City .
        ?city rdfs:label ?cityLabel . FILTER(lang(?cityLabel)='en')
      }
      ?river rdfs:label ?riverLabel . FILTER(lang(?riverLabel)='en')
    }
    ORDER BY ?riverLabel ?cityLabel
    		 
    {}
    		 
    {
        "set_P": 0.0,
        "set_recall": 0.0,
        "set_F": 0.0
    }
    db26:20-es 
    ¿Por qué ciudades fluyen los ríos
    que se originan en el Parque
    Nacional de Yellowstone?
    		 
    SELECT DISTINCT ?uri WHERE {
      ?x <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/sourcePlace> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Yellowstone_National_Park> .
      ?x <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/city> ?uri .
      ?x <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#type> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/River> .
    }

        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Lewiston,_Idaho": 1,

    SELECT DISTINCT ?river ?riverLabel ?src ?srcLabel ?city ?cityLabel WHERE {

    db26:21-en 
    Which alliance has the most member
    airlines?
    		 
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    SELECT ?uri
    WHERE {
      ?airline a dbo:Airline .
      ?airline dbp:alliance ?uri .
    }
    GROUP BY ?uri
    ORDER BY DESC(COUNT(DISTINCT ?airline))
    OFFSET 0 LIMIT 1
    		 
    {
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Star_Alliance": 1
    }
    		 
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
    SELECT ?alliance (SAMPLE(?label) AS ?label) (COUNT(DISTINCT ?airline) AS ?members) WHERE {
      {
        ?airline rdf:type dbo:Airline .
        ?airline (dbo:alliance | dbp:alliance) ?alliance .
      }
      UNION
      {
        ?alliance (dbo:member | dbp:member) ?airline .
        ?airline rdf:type dbo:Airline .
      }
      OPTIONAL { ?alliance rdfs:label ?label FILTER(lang(?label)='en') }
    }
    GROUP BY ?alliance
    ORDER BY DESC(?members)
    LIMIT 1
    		 
    {}

    db26:22-en 
    Which companies built the stadium
    whose tenant was the BC Lions?
    		 
    SELECT DISTINCT ?uri WHERE {
      ?x <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/tenant> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/BC_Lions> .
      ?x <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/builder> ?uri .
      ?x a <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/Stadium> .
      ?uri a <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/Company> .
    }
    		 
    {
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Nussli_Group": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/PCL_Construction": 1
    }
    		 
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?stadium ?stadiumLabel ?builder ?builderLabel WHERE {
      VALUES ?team { dbr:BC_Lions }
      # find stadiums where the BC Lions are tenants (cover dbo/dbp variants)
      ?stadium (dbo:tenant|dbp:tenant|dbp:tenants|dbp:formerTenants) ?team .
      # try to find builder/constructor/contractor companies via multiple property variants
      OPTIONAL {
        ?stadium (dbo:builder|dbp:builtBy|dbp:builder|dbp:contractor|dbp:generalcontractor|dbo:architect|dbp:architect) ?builder .
        FILTER(isIRI(?builder))
      }
      OPTIONAL { ?stadium rdfs:label ?stadiumLabel FILTER(lang(?stadiumLabel)='en') }
      OPTIONAL { ?builder rdfs:label ?builderLabel FILTER(lang(?builderLabel)='en') }
    }
    ORDER BY ?stadium ?builder

    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?predecessor ?predecessorLabel ?party ?partyLabel WHERE {
      { ?predecessor dbo:successor dbr:Yingluck_Shinawatra } UNION { ?predecessor dbp:successor dbr:Yingluck_Shinawatra }
      OPTIONAL { ?predecessor rdfs:label ?predecessorLabel FILTER(lang(?predecessorLabel)='en') }
      OPTIONAL { ?predecessor dbo:party ?party }
      OPTIONAL { ?predecessor dbp:party ?party }
      OPTIONAL { ?party rdfs:label ?partyLabel FILTER(lang(?partyLabel)='en') }
    } LIMIT 10

    Which television shows were
    produced through a collaboration
    between HBO and BBC?
    		 
    SELECT DISTINCT ?uri
    WHERE {
      ?uri <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/company> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/HBO> .
      ?uri <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/company> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/BBC> .
      ?uri <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#type> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/TelevisionShow> .
    }
    		 
    {
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Family_Tree_(TV_series)": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/The_Girl_(2012_TV_film)": 1
    }
    		 
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?show ?label WHERE {
      VALUES ?hbo { dbr:HBO }
      VALUES ?bbc { dbr:BBC }

      ?show (dbo:productionCompany|dbo:company|dbp:coProduction|dbp:company|dbp:productionCompany) ?hbo .
      ?show (dbo:productionCompany|dbo:company|dbp:coProduction|dbp:company|dbp:productionCompany) ?bbc .

      OPTIONAL { ?show rdfs:label ?label FILTER(lang(?label)='en') }
      FILTER(?show != ?hbo && ?show != ?bbc)
    } ORDER BY ?label

        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/The_Girl_(2012_TV_film)": 1,

      ?uri <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/company> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/BBC> .

    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?show ?label WHERE {
      ?show ?p dbr:HBO .
      ?show ?q dbr:BBC .
      OPTIONAL { ?show rdfs:label ?label . FILTER(lang(?label) IN ('es','en')) }
      FILTER(
        EXISTS{ ?show rdf:type dbo:TelevisionShow } ||
        EXISTS{ ?show rdf:type dbo:TelevisionSeries } ||
        EXISTS{ ?show rdf:type dbo:TelevisionEpisode } ||
        regex(str(?label),'TV','i') || regex(str(?label),'miniseries','i') || regex(str(?label),'television','i')
      )
    }
    ORDER BY ?label

    db26:26-en 
    List all the publishers of the
    Oddworld series.
    		 
    SELECT DISTINCT ?uri
    WHERE {
      ?x <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/series> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Oddworld> .
      ?x <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/publisher> ?uri .
    }
    		 
    {
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Electronic_Arts": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Oddworld_Inhabitants": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/GT_Interactive_Software": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Riverhillsoft": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Sony_Computer_Entertainment": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/THQ": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Microsoft": 1
    }
    		 
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?publisher ?publisherLabel WHERE {
      VALUES ?series { dbr:Oddworld }
      {
        ?game dbp:series ?series .
      } UNION {
        ?game dbo:series ?series .
      }
      OPTIONAL { ?game dbo:publisher ?publisherIRI . }
      OPTIONAL { ?game dbp:publisher ?publisherIRI2 . }
      BIND(COALESCE(?publisherIRI, ?publisherIRI2) AS ?publisherRaw)
      BIND(IF(isIRI(?publisherRaw), ?publisherRaw, STR(?publisherRaw)) AS ?publisher)
      OPTIONAL {
        FILTER(isIRI(?publisher))
        ?publisher rdfs:label ?publisherLabel . FILTER(lang(?publisherLabel) = 'en')
      }
    }
    ORDER BY ?publisherLabel
    		 
    {}

        "set_recall": 0.0,
        "set_F": 0.0

    WHERE {

      ?x <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/publisher> ?uri .
    }
    		 
    {
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Electronic_Arts": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Oddworld_Inhabitants": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/GT_Interactive_Software": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Riverhillsoft": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Sony_Computer_Entertainment": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/THQ": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Microsoft": 1
    }
    		 
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?publisher ?publisherLabel WHERE {
      ?game rdfs:label ?glabel .
      FILTER(LANG(?glabel) = 'en')
      FILTER(CONTAINS(LCASE(?glabel), "oddworld"))
      ?game rdf:type ?type .
      FILTER(?type IN (dbo:VideoGame, dbo:Software, dbo:Work))
      { ?game dbo:publisher ?publisher } UNION { ?game dbp:publisher ?publisher } UNION { ?game dbp:publishedBy ?publisher }
      OPTIONAL { ?publisher rdfs:label ?publisherLabel . FILTER(LANG(?publisherLabel)='en') }
    } ORDER BY ?publisherLabel

    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?film ?filmLabel WHERE {
      { ?film dbo:musicComposer dbr:Ennio_Morricone . }
      UNION { ?film dbp:musicComposer dbr:Ennio_Morricone . }
      UNION { ?film dbo:composer dbr:Ennio_Morricone . }
      UNION { ?film dbp:music dbr:Ennio_Morricone . }
      ?film rdf:type ?type .
      FILTER(?type IN (dbo:Film, dbo:Work))
      OPTIONAL { ?film rdfs:label ?filmLabel . FILTER(lang(?filmLabel)='en') }
    } ORDER BY ?filmLabel

    SELECT DISTINCT ?film ?filmLabel WHERE {
      ?film (dbo:musicComposer|dbp:musicComposer|dbp:musicComposers|dbp:musicDirectorAndComposer) dbr:Ennio_Morricone .
      OPTIONAL { ?film rdfs:label ?filmLabel FILTER(lang(?filmLabel) = 'en') }
    } ORDER BY ?filmLabel

    Name some television shows related
    to the one whose opening theme was
    composed by Bear McCreary.
    		 
    SELECT DISTINCT ?uri WHERE {
      ?x <http://dbpedia.org/property/themeMusicComposer> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Bear_McCreary> .
      ?uri <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/related> ?x .
      ?uri <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#type> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/TelevisionShow> .
    }
    		 
    {
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Fear_the_Walking_Dead": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Talking_Dead": 1
    }
    		 
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?origShow ?origShowLabel ?related ?relatedLabel WHERE {
      VALUES ?composer { dbr:Bear_McCreary }
      ?origShow rdf:type ?type .
      FILTER EXISTS { ?type rdfs:label ?tlabel FILTER(regex(str(?tlabel),'Television','i')) }
      ?origShow (dbo:musicComposer|dbp:musicComposer|dbp:themeMusicComposer|dbo:composer|dbp:composer) ?composer .
      OPTIONAL { ?origShow dbo:related ?related . }
      OPTIONAL { ?origShow dbp:related ?related . }
      FILTER(bound(?related))
      OPTIONAL { ?origShow rdfs:label ?origShowLabel FILTER(lang(?origShowLabel)='en') }
      OPTIONAL { ?related rdfs:label ?relatedLabel FILTER(lang(?relatedLabel)='en') }
    } LIMIT 50

    db26:29-es 
    ¿Dónde fue grabada la canción
    'Imagine'?
    		 
    SELECT DISTINCT ?uri WHERE {
      <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Imagine_(John_Lennon_song)> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/recordedIn> ?uri
    }
    		 
    {
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Tittenhurst_Park": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Record_Plant": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/New_York": 1
    }
    		 
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?place ?placeLabel WHERE {
      VALUES ?song { <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Imagine_(John_Lennon_song)> }
      {
        ?song dbo:recordedIn ?place .
      } UNION {
        ?song dbp:recorded ?place .
      } UNION {
        ?song dbo:recorded ?place .
      }
      FILTER(isIRI(?place))
      OPTIONAL { ?place rdfs:label ?placeLabel . FILTER(lang(?placeLabel) = 'en') }
    }
    		 
    {
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Tittenhurst_Park": 1,

    ¿Dónde estudió el cónyuge de Aung
    San Suu Kyi?
    		 
    SELECT DISTINCT ?uri WHERE {
      <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Aung_San_Suu_Kyi> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/spouse> ?x .
      ?x <http://dbpedia.org/property/almaMater> ?uri .
    }
    		 
    {
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/SOAS,_University_of_London": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Worth_School": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Durham_University": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/St_Cuthbert's_Society,_Durham": 1
    }
    		 
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?spouse ?spouseLabel ?study ?studyLabel WHERE {
      VALUES ?person { dbr:Aung_San_Suu_Kyi }
      # spouse via dbo:spouse or dbp:spouse
      { ?person dbo:spouse ?spouse } UNION { ?person dbp:spouse ?spouse }
      # education/alma mater via several common predicates; allow literals too
      OPTIONAL { ?spouse dbo:almaMater ?study . }
      OPTIONAL { ?spouse dbo:education ?study . }
      OPTIONAL { ?spouse dbp:almaMater ?study . }
      OPTIONAL { ?spouse dbp:education ?study . }
      # if study is literal return it as a literal node; otherwise get label
      OPTIONAL { ?spouse rdfs:label ?spouseLabel FILTER(lang(?spouseLabel)='en') }
      OPTIONAL { ?study rdfs:label ?studyLabel FILTER(lang(?studyLabel)='en') }
      # also prefer to capture literal literal studies from dbp:* as ?studyLabel when ?study is a literal
      BIND(IF(isLiteral(?study), STR(?study), ?studyLabel) AS ?studyLabelFinal)
    } LIMIT 50

    WHERE {

      <http://dbpedia.org/resource/University_of_Hawaii_at_Manoa> dbo:city ?uri .
    }
    		 
    {
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Honolulu": 1
    }
    		 
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?place ?placeLabel WHERE {
      VALUES ?person { dbr:Barack_Obama }
      VALUES ?univ { dbr:University_of_Hawaii_at_Manoa }

      { ?person dbo:birthPlace ?place } UNION { ?person dbp:birthPlace ?place }

      {
        ?univ dbo:city ?place
      } UNION {
        ?univ dbp:city ?place
      } UNION {
        ?univ dbo:location ?place
      } UNION {
        ?univ dbp:location ?place
      } .

      OPTIONAL { ?place rdfs:label ?placeLabel FILTER(lang(?placeLabel) = 'en') }
    } LIMIT 20

        ?structure dbp:designer ?person .
      } UNION {

      }
      {
        ?structure dbo:crosses ?river .
      } UNION {
        ?structure dbp:crosses ?river .
      }
      FILTER(isIRI(?river))
    }

    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>

      VALUES ?lang { dbr:Portuguese_language }

      {
        ?country dbo:officialLanguage ?lang .
      } UNION {
        ?country dbp:officialLanguage ?lang .
      } UNION {
        ?country dbp:officialLanguages ?lang .
      }
    } ORDER BY ?countryLabel

        { ?person dbo:spouse dbr:Angelina_Jolie }
        UNION
        { dbr:Angelina_Jolie dbo:spouse ?person }
        UNION
        { ?person dbp:spouse dbr:Angelina_Jolie }
        UNION
        { dbr:Angelina_Jolie dbp:spouse ?person }
      }
      OPTIONAL { ?person rdfs:label ?personLabel FILTER(lang(?personLabel) = 'en') }

    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?software (SAMPLE(?label) AS ?label) WHERE {
      ?software rdf:type dbo:Software .
      ?software (dbo:operatingSystem|dbp:operatingSystem) ?os .
      ?os rdfs:label ?osLabel FILTER(lang(?osLabel)='en') .
      ?software rdfs:label ?label FILTER(lang(?label)='en') .
    }
    GROUP BY ?software
    HAVING(
      SUM(IF(CONTAINS(LCASE(?osLabel),'linux'),1,0))>0
      && SUM(IF(CONTAINS(LCASE(?osLabel),'mac'),1,0))>0
    )
    ORDER BY ?label

    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?software ?softwareLabel WHERE {
      ?software (dbo:operatingSystem|dbp:operatingSystem|dbp:operatingSystems) ?os1 .
      ?software (dbo:operatingSystem|dbp:operatingSystem|dbp:operatingSystems) ?os2 .
      ?os1 rdfs:label ?os1Label .
      ?os2 rdfs:label ?os2Label .
      FILTER(regex(lcase(str(?os1Label)),"linux"))
      FILTER(regex(lcase(str(?os2Label)),"mac|os x|mac os|macos"))
      OPTIONAL { ?software rdfs:label ?softwareLabel FILTER(lang(?softwareLabel)='es') }
      OPTIONAL { ?software rdfs:label ?softwareLabel FILTER(lang(?softwareLabel)='en') }
    } ORDER BY ?softwareLabel LIMIT 200

    SELECT DISTINCT ?aff (SAMPLE(?affLabel) AS ?affLabelSample) WHERE {
      VALUES ?metro { dbr:Metro_Manila }
      ?uni rdf:type dbo:University .
      { ?uni dbo:location ?metro } UNION { ?uni dbo:locatedInArea ?metro } UNION { ?uni dbo:isPartOf ?metro } UNION { ?uni dbp:location ?loc FILTER(regex(lcase(str(?loc)), "metro manila")) }
      OPTIONAL { ?uni (dbo:affiliation|dbp:affiliation|dbp:academicAffiliation) ?aff }
      OPTIONAL { ?aff rdfs:label ?affLabel FILTER(lang(?affLabel) = 'en') }
    }
    GROUP BY ?aff
    ORDER BY ?affLabelSample

    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?otherAward ?otherAwardLabel WHERE {
      ?person a dbo:Person .
      {
        ?person dbo:award dbr:Presidential_Medal_of_Freedom .
      } UNION {
        ?person dbp:award dbr:Presidential_Medal_of_Freedom .
      } UNION {
        ?person dbp:awards dbr:Presidential_Medal_of_Freedom .
      } UNION {
        ?person dbp:honors dbr:Presidential_Medal_of_Freedom .
      }

      # other awards the same person has
      {
        ?person dbo:award ?otherAward .
      } UNION {
        ?person dbp:award ?otherAward .
      } UNION {
        ?person dbp:awards ?otherAward .
      } UNION {
        ?person dbp:honors ?otherAward .
      }

      FILTER(?otherAward != dbr:Presidential_Medal_of_Freedom)
      OPTIONAL { ?otherAward rdfs:label ?otherAwardLabel FILTER(lang(?otherAwardLabel) = 'en') }
    } ORDER BY ?otherAwardLabel

    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?recipient ?recipientLabel ?otherAward ?otherAwardLabel WHERE {
      VALUES ?medal { dbr:Presidential_Medal_of_Freedom }
      {
        ?recipient (dbo:award|dbp:awards) ?medal .
      }
      ?recipient rdf:type dbo:Person .
      ?recipient rdfs:label ?recipientLabel FILTER(lang(?recipientLabel)='en')
      ?recipient (dbo:award|dbp:awards|dbp:honours|dbp:honors) ?otherAward .
      FILTER(?otherAward != ?medal)
      OPTIONAL { ?otherAward rdfs:label ?otherAwardLabel FILTER(lang(?otherAwardLabel)='en') }
    }
    LIMIT 200

    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>

    SELECT DISTINCT ?saint ?labelEN ?labelES WHERE {
      ?saint rdf:type dbo:Saint .
      {
        ?saint dbo:veneratedIn dbr:Christianity .
      } UNION {
        ?saint dbp:veneratedIn dbr:Christianity .
      } UNION {
        ?saint dbp:venerated dbr:Christianity .
      } UNION {
        ?saint dbo:veneratedIn ?church .
        ?church rdfs:label ?churchLabel .
        FILTER(LCASE(STR(?churchLabel)) = "christianity"@en || regex(LCASE(STR(?churchLabel)), "christianity"))
      }
      OPTIONAL { ?saint rdfs:label ?labelEN . FILTER(lang(?labelEN) = 'en') }
      OPTIONAL { ?saint rdfs:label ?labelES . FILTER(lang(?labelES) = 'es') }
    } LIMIT 200

    db26:46-en 
    Which city is the radio station
    located in that is a sister station
    of WXME?
    		 
    SELECT DISTINCT ?uri WHERE {
      <http://dbpedia.org/resource/WXME> <http://dbpedia.org/property/sisterStations> ?x .
      ?x <http://dbpedia.org/property/city> ?uri .
      ?x <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#type> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/RadioStation> .
    }
    		 
    {
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Maine": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Monticello,_Maine": 1
    }
    		 
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?sister ?sisterLabel ?city ?cityLabel WHERE {
      VALUES ?station { dbr:WXME }
      {
        ?station dbo:sisterStation ?sister .
      } UNION {
        ?station dbp:sisterStations ?sister .
      }
      OPTIONAL { ?sister dbo:city ?city . }
      OPTIONAL { ?sister dbo:location ?city . }
      OPTIONAL { ?sister dbp:city ?city . }
      OPTIONAL { ?sister dbo:licensedToServe ?city . }
      OPTIONAL { ?sister dbp:licensedToServe ?city . }
      OPTIONAL { ?sister rdfs:label ?sisterLabel . FILTER(lang(?sisterLabel)='en') }
      OPTIONAL { ?city rdfs:label ?cityLabel . FILTER(lang(?cityLabel)='en') }
    }
    		 
    {
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/WBCQ_(SW)": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/WBCQ-FM": 1,
        "WBCQ (SW)": 1,
        "WBCQ-FM": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Maine": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Monticello,_Maine": 1,
        "Maine": 1,
        "Monticello, Maine": 1
    }

        "set_F": 0.4

    }
    		 
    {
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Maine": 1,
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Monticello,_Maine": 1
    }
    		 
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?s ?sLabel ?city ?cityLabel WHERE {
      VALUES ?me { dbr:WXME }
      {
        ?me dbo:sisterStation ?s .
      } UNION {
        ?me dbp:sisterStations ?s .
      }
      OPTIONAL { ?s rdfs:label ?sLabel . FILTER(lang(?sLabel) = 'en') }
      OPTIONAL {
        { ?s dbo:city ?city } UNION { ?s dbp:city ?city } UNION { ?s dbo:location ?city }
        OPTIONAL { ?city rdfs:label ?cityLabel . FILTER(lang(?cityLabel) = 'en') }
      }
    }

    db26:47-en 
    What is the name of the river that
    has its source in Lake Itasca and
    its river mouth located in Gulf of
    Mexico?
    		 
    SELECT DISTINCT ?uri
    WHERE {
      ?uri <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/riverMouth> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Gulf_of_Mexico> .
      ?uri <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/source> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Lake_Itasca> .
      ?uri <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#type> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/River> .
    }
    		 
    {
        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Mississippi_River": 1
    }
    		 
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?river WHERE {
      ?river rdf:type ?t .
      ?river rdfs:comment ?c .
      FILTER(regex(str(?c), "Lake Itasca", "i") && regex(str(?c), "Gulf of Mexico", "i"))
      FILTER(EXISTS { ?river dbo:type dbr:River } || EXISTS { ?river dbo:river ?any } || EXISTS { ?river dbo:mouth ?m } )
    } LIMIT 10

    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?river ?label WHERE {
      VALUES ?lake { dbr:Lake_Itasca }
      VALUES ?sea  { dbr:Gulf_of_Mexico }

      {
        ?river (dbo:source|dbo:sourceLocation|dbp:source) ?lake .
      }
      UNION
      {
        ?river (dbo:origin|dbp:origin) ?lake .
      }

      {
        ?river (dbo:mouth|dbo:mouthLocation|dbp:mouth) ?sea .
      }
      UNION
      {
        ?river (dbo:dischargeLocation|dbp:discharge) ?sea .
      }

      OPTIONAL { ?river rdfs:label ?label . FILTER(lang(?label) IN ('en','es')) }
    } LIMIT 10

      ?x <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/formerTeam> ?uri .
    }

    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT (COUNT(DISTINCT ?other) AS ?count) WHERE {
      # players who have (at some point) played for HC Slovan Bratislava
      {
        ?player dbo:team dbr:HC_Slovan_Bratislava .
      } UNION {
        ?player dbp:team dbr:HC_Slovan_Bratislava .
      } UNION {
        ?player dbp:teams dbr:HC_Slovan_Bratislava .
      }

      # other teams these players have played for (capture multiple modeling variants)
      {
        ?player dbo:team ?other .
      } UNION {
        ?player dbp:team ?other .
      } UNION {
        ?player dbp:teams ?other .
      }

      FILTER(isIRI(?other))
      FILTER(?other != dbr:HC_Slovan_Bratislava)
    }

    db26:48-es 
    ¿Para cuántos otros equipos han
    jugado los miembros del HC Slovan
    Bratislava?
    		 
    SELECT (COUNT(DISTINCT ?uri) as ?uriCount) WHERE {
      ?x <http://dbpedia.org/property/playedFor> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/HC_Slovan_Bratislava> .
      ?x <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/formerTeam> ?uri .
    }

    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT ?player (SAMPLE(?labelEN) AS ?label) (COUNT(DISTINCT ?otherTeam) AS ?otherTeams) WHERE {
      ?player a dbo:Person .
      {
        ?player dbo:team dbr:HC_Slovan_Bratislava .
      } UNION {
        ?player dbp:team dbr:HC_Slovan_Bratislava .
      } UNION {
        ?player dbp:formerTeam dbr:HC_Slovan_Bratislava .
      } UNION {
        ?player dbo:formerTeam dbr:HC_Slovan_Bratislava .
      }
      {
        ?player dbo:team ?otherTeam .
      } UNION {
        ?player dbp:team ?otherTeam .
      } UNION {
        ?player dbp:formerTeam ?otherTeam .
      } UNION {
        ?player dbo:formerTeam ?otherTeam .
      }
      FILTER(?otherTeam != dbr:HC_Slovan_Bratislava)
      OPTIONAL { ?player rdfs:label ?labelEN FILTER(lang(?labelEN)='en') }
    } GROUP BY ?player
    ORDER BY DESC(?otherTeams)

    db26:49-en 
    What is the population difference
    between Tokyo and Delhi?
    		 
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT DISTINCT (?p2 - ?p1 AS ?result)
    WHERE {
      <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Tokyo> dbo:populationTotal ?p1 .
      <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Delhi> dbo:populationTotal ?p2 .
    }
    		 
    {
        "3129336": 1
    }
    		 
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
    SELECT ?tokyo ?delhi ?popTokyo ?popDelhi (?popDelhi - ?popTokyo AS ?populationDifference) WHERE {
      BIND(dbr:Tokyo AS ?tokyo)
      BIND(dbr:Delhi AS ?delhi)
      OPTIONAL { ?tokyo dbo:populationTotal ?tokyo_dbo . }
      OPTIONAL { ?tokyo dbp:populationTotal ?tokyo_dbp . }
      OPTIONAL { ?tokyo dbo:populationMetro ?tokyo_metro . }
      OPTIONAL { ?tokyo dbp:populationMetro ?tokyo_metro_dbp . }
      OPTIONAL { ?delhi dbo:populationTotal ?delhi_dbo . }
      OPTIONAL { ?delhi dbp:populationTotal ?delhi_dbp . }
      OPTIONAL { ?delhi dbo:populationMetro ?delhi_metro . }
      OPTIONAL { ?delhi dbp:populationMetro ?delhi_metro_dbp . }
      BIND(COALESCE(xsd:integer(?tokyo_dbo), xsd:integer(?tokyo_dbp), xsd:integer(?tokyo_metro), xsd:integer(?tokyo_metro_dbp)) AS ?popTokyo)
      BIND(COALESCE(xsd:integer(?delhi_dbo), xsd:integer(?delhi_dbp), xsd:integer(?delhi_metro), xsd:integer(?delhi_metro_dbp)) AS ?popDelhi)
    }
    LIMIT 1

        "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Delhi": 1,

    db26:49-es 
    ¿Cuál es la diferencia de población
    entre Tokio y Delhi?
    		 
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT DISTINCT (?p2 - ?p1 AS ?result)
    WHERE {
      <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Tokyo> dbo:populationTotal ?p1 .
      <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Delhi> dbo:populationTotal ?p2 .
    }
    		 
    {
        "3129336": 1
    }
    		 
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
    SELECT ?tokyo ?delhi ?popTokyo ?popDelhi (ABS(xsd:integer(?popTokyo) - xsd:integer(?popDelhi)) AS ?difference) WHERE {
      VALUES ?tokyo { dbr:Tokyo }
      VALUES ?delhi { dbr:Delhi }

      OPTIONAL { ?tokyo dbo:populationTotal ?p1. }
      OPTIONAL { ?tokyo dbp:populationTotal ?p1b. }
      OPTIONAL { ?tokyo dbo:populationMetro ?p1c. }
      BIND( COALESCE(?p1, ?p1b, ?p1c) AS ?popTokyo )

      OPTIONAL { ?delhi dbo:populationTotal ?q1. }
      OPTIONAL { ?delhi dbp:populationTotal ?q1b. }
      OPTIONAL { ?delhi dbo:populationMetro ?q1c. }
      BIND( COALESCE(?q1, ?q1b, ?q1c) AS ?popDelhi )

      FILTER(BOUND(?popTokyo) && BOUND(?popDelhi))
    }LIMIT 1

    db26:50-en 
    How many movies there are in French
    language?
    		 
    SELECT (COUNT(DISTINCT ?uri) as ?uriCount) WHERE {
      ?uri <http://dbpedia.org/property/language> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/French_language> .
      ?uri <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#type> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/Film>
    }
    		 
    {
        "789": 1
    }
    		 
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
    SELECT (COUNT(DISTINCT ?film) AS ?count) WHERE {
      ?film rdf:type ?t .
      VALUES ?t { dbo:Film dbo:Work }
      {
        ?film dbo:language dbr:French_language .
      } UNION {
        ?film dbp:language dbr:French_language .
      } UNION {
        ?film dbp:language ?langLit .
        FILTER(regex(lcase(str(?langLit)), "french"))
      } UNION {
        ?film dbo:language ?langLit2 .
        FILTER(regex(lcase(str(?langLit2)), "french"))
      }
    }

    }
    		 
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    SELECT (COUNT(DISTINCT ?film) AS ?count) WHERE {
      ?film a ?type .
      FILTER(?type IN (dbo:Film, dbo:Work)) .
      ?film (dbo:language|dbp:language) ?lang .
      FILTER( ?lang = dbr:French_language || (isLiteral(?lang) && regex(str(?lang), "French", "i")) )
    }
    		 
    {
        "7406": 1
    }
    		 
    {
        "set_P": 0.0,
        "set_recall": 0.0,
        "set_F": 0.0
    }
